## createHrisAttendance

Creates a HrisAttendance. Provide the unified HrisAttendance fields in the request body; they are mapped to the provider's API. Returns the created HrisAttendance.

### Example Usage

<!-- UsageSnippet language="typescript" operationID="createHrisAttendance" method="post" path="/hris/{connection_id}/attendance" example="hris_attendance" -->
```typescript
import { UnifiedTo } from "@unified-api/typescript-sdk";

const unifiedTo = new UnifiedTo({
  security: {
    jwt: "<YOUR_API_KEY_HERE>",
  },
});

async function run() {
  const result = await unifiedTo.hris.createHrisAttendance({
    hrisAttendance: {
      address: {
        address1: "18002 Elm Street",
        city: "Port Gaetano",
        countryCode: "US",
        postalCode: "42344",
        region: "Vermont",
        regionCode: "MI",
      },
      approvedAt: new Date("2021-09-20T08:09:41.017Z"),
      breaks: [
        {
          durationMinutes: 51,
          endAt: new Date("2024-03-02T21:53:43.030Z"),
          id: "87808cad-e25c-4df7-a1ff-aeecadc6b45d",
          isPaid: false,
          name: "Lunch",
          startAt: new Date("2023-11-14T06:50:02.982Z"),
        },
      ],
      createdAt: new Date("2021-08-10T19:43:18.452Z"),
      currency: "EUR",
      declaredTipsAmount: 54,
      employeeUserId: "<id>",
      endAt: new Date("2024-05-11T23:39:10.092Z"),
      hourlyRate: 16,
      hours: 10,
      id: "2bbaa0bc-a8a9-4b46-abdb-97452cb34d1a",
      jobName: "Human Accountability Administrator",
      metadata: [],
      nonCashTipsAmount: 167,
      startAt: new Date("2021-11-12T18:47:53.825Z"),
      status: "OPEN",
      timezone: "Africa/Ouagadougou",
      updatedAt: new Date("2022-01-22T22:38:46.235Z"),
    },
    connectionId: "<id>",
  });

  console.log(result);
}

run();
```
